465	/-- **`prog_sim`** — if the D7/D8 IL says `entry(args)` computes `s'` (with the
466	    P1 padding `userPad`), then the compiled RV64I blob, started at `codeBase`
467	    with `args` in `a0..` and `sp = sp0`, runs to the halt pad in a state whose
468	    `a0..` hold `entry`'s return values and whose memory agrees with `s'`
469	    everywhere outside the blob and the stack. Const data is placed at
470	    `codeBase + segStart` on BOTH sides (`dataOffsetsFrom_shift`). -/
471	theorem prog_sim
472	    {P : Program} {entry : Name} {fd : FunDef} {args : List Word}
473	    {stackLo sp0 : Word} {fuel : Nat} {s' : St} {L : Layout} {m0 : State}
474	    (hlk    : List.lookup entry P.env = some fd)
475	    (hL     : layoutOf P entry L.codeBase L.stackLo = some L)
476	    (hpre   : SimPre L stackLo sp0)
477	    (hpc    : m0.pc = L.codeBase)
478	    (hsp    : m0.rget 2 = sp0)
479	    (hargs  : ∀ i, i < fd.argc → m0.rget (10 + i) = args.getD i 0)
480	    (hinst  : Installed L m0)
481	    (hmem   : ∀ a, ¬ MachPriv L [] a →
482	                installData (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 L.segStart) P.data (fun _ => 0) a
483	                  = m0.mem a)
484	    (hrun   : LowIR.Prog.run P stackLo fuel entry args (fun _ => 0) sp0
485	                (userPad P.env) (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 L.segStart) = some s') :
486	    ∃ k, (Rv64i.runFuel L.haltPc k m0).pc = L.haltPc
487	       ∧ (∀ j, j < fd.rvc →
488	            (Rv64i.runFuel L.haltPc k m0).rget (10 + j) = s'.rget (fd.rets.toList.getD j 0))
489	       ∧ (∀ a, ¬ memRange a L.codeBase L.blobLen → ¬ MachStack stackLo sp0 a →
490	            s'.mem a = (Rv64i.runFuel L.haltPc k m0).mem a) := by

## 4. Proof architecture — phases, deliverables, risk

**Phase 0 — P1 + toolkit.** (blocks everything)
1. Implement P1: add the `pad : Name → Nat` oracle to `frameEnter`/`exec`/
   `run` (default `fun _ => 0` — everything existing re-greens unchanged;
   the compiler, shim, and QEMU artifacts are untouched and hereby FROZEN).
   Add the new differential tests with `pad := userOff`-instantiated IL
   runs comparing FRAME memory byte-for-byte — the executable validation of
   the whole P1 idea, before any lemma depends on it. Commit.
2. Port the one-layer unfolder lemmas to Prog: `exec_seq_normal`,
   `exec_block_*`, `exec_while_*`, `exec_call_*`, per-op equations
   (from `CtrlStrtoullProof.lean`, mostly mechanical renames + dbase/env
   params). NEVER `simp [exec] at h` with an IH in context (OOM — see the
   gotcha memory); these lemmas exist to make that unnecessary.
3. `execT` + erasure (§3.4).
   *Risk: low. Size: ~400 lines.*

**Phase 1 — encode/decode generically.** `decode (encode i) = i` per
constructor, ∀ operands in range (rd/rs < 32, sh < 64, imm any). Today this
is only checked per-program by `native_decide`. Two routes: (a) restate
`encode`'s field packing in BitVec form and hit each constructor with
`bv_decide`/`bv_omega` after bounding Nat operands; (b) hand bit-arithmetic
à la `RvCross.v`'s decode half. Try (a) first; budget for (b).
*Risk: MEDIUM (known painful bit-fiddling). Size: ~600 lines. Fully
parallel to Phases 2-3; can start immediately.*

**Phase 2 — the assembler layer (layout/resolve).**
- `symSize`-consistency: `(resolveOne … (pos,si)).length * 4 = symSize si`;
  `progBytes` indexing: instruction j's 4 bytes sit at its `layout` position
  (list-flatten arithmetic, same flavor as the already-proved
  `dataSegment_at` — reuse its induction pattern).
- Label soundness: `fresh` monotonicity ⇒ label nodup ⇒ `lbls.lookup` is THE
  address; every emitted `.br/.jmp/.callf/.cref` resolves to a target that
  is a real position in the stream (br within the function, callf to
  `fnTab`, cref to `segStart + dataOffsetsFrom` — `dataOffsetsFrom_shift`
  closes the loop).
- Immediate roundtrips under the resolve range checks:
  `(BitVec.ofInt 13 δ).signExtend 64 = BitVec.ofInt 64 δ` for
  `-4096 ≤ δ ≤ 4094`, ditto 21-bit; `synthConst_correct` (3 steps compute
  `ofInt v`, |v| < 2²³) and the `cref` 5-step lemma including the
  **jal-pc-read** equation (`jal rd, +4` ⇒ rd := pc+4, pc := pc+4).
- `Installed`-from-`progBytes` + preservation under off-code stores
  (hex0's `code_preserved` pattern).
  *Risk: low-medium, high volume. Size: ~1000 lines.*

**Phase 3 — StInv algebra ("slot facts").** Slot read/write lemmas: store
to slot r updates `loadWord` at r, preserves other slots (8-byte
disjointness arithmetic), preserves `Installed` and off-MachPriv memory;
`rget/rset` ↔ slot-store correspondence incl. the r=0 discard/li-0 cases;
x3 untouched by op lowerings. This is `li_block_frame`-style plumbing —
tedious, well-understood.
*Risk: low. Size: ~600 lines.*

**Phase 4 — `lower_sim`, non-call cases.** The big fuel induction, cases in
this order (each case = commit):
1. skip/annot, ops (arith, shifts) — validates the whole pipeline on
   4-instruction slices. **Do this as the VERTICAL SLICE first**: state
   everything in §3, prove only these cases, leave the rest `sorry`, and
   check a toy end-to-end corollary (sub3-class) against the differential
   oracle. If the relation is wrong, it is wrong HERE, cheaply.
2. IL memory ops (lbu/sb/ld/sd) — first footprint use; address equality is
   the P1 payoff (IL address = machine address, literally).
3. seq (outcome-threading composition), ife (branch resolve + join),
   block/brkB/contL/ret (pure pcOut bookkeeping), while (fuel IH).
4. cref/clen — `synthConst_correct` + `installData_at`/`Installed`-data.
   *Risk: medium. Size: ~1500 lines.*

**Phase 5 — the call case + `call_sim`.** The summit. Sub-lemmas:
- `prologue_sim`: machine prologue from a call-entry state establis

261	/-! ### Assemble + run on the trusted `Rv64i` machine. -/
262	
263	/-- Little-endian 4 bytes of an instruction. -/
264	def insBytes (i : Instr) : List Byte :=
265	  let w := encode i
266	  [w.setWidth 8, (w >>> 8).setWidth 8, (w >>> 16).setWidth 8, (w >>> 24).setWidth 8]

268	def asmBytes (is : List Instr) : List Byte := (is.map insBytes).flatten
269	
270	/-- Memory holding `code` at `codeBase` and `data` at `dataBase` (disjoint). -/
271	def loadMem (codeBase : Word) (code : List Byte) (dataBase : Word) (data : List Byte) : Word → Byte :=
272	  fun a =>
273	    let ca := (a - codeBase).toNat
274	    if ca < code.length then (code[ca]?).getD 0
275	    else (data[(a - dataBase).toNat]?).getD 0
276	
277	/-- Initial RV64I state: program assembled at `codeBase`, string at `dataBase`,
278	    x10 = dataBase (the argument). -/
279	def mkState (codeBase dataBase : Word) (p : Stmt) (data : List Byte) : Rv64i.State :=
280	  { reg := fun i => if i = 10 then dataBase else 0
281	    pc  := codeBase
282	    mem := loadMem codeBase (asmBytes (compile p)) dataBase data }
283	
284	/-- Run the compiled program on `Rv64i` until it falls off the end. -/
285	def rvRun (codeBase dataBase : Word) (p : Stmt) (data : List Byte) (fuel : Nat) : Rv64i.State :=
286	  let halt := codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * (compile p).length)
287	  Rv64i.runFuel halt fuel (mkState codeBase dataBase p data)

85	  let immS   : BitVec 12 := BitVec.ofNat 12 ((funct7 <<< 5) ||| rd)
86	  -- B-type immediate: [12]=b31 [11]=b7 [10:5]=b[30:25] [4:1]=b[11:8] [0]=0

92	      (((field w 31 1) <<< 20) ||| ((field w 12 8) <<< 12) |||

75	/-! ## `Emitted` — the static "code segment carries `is` at `pos`" fact. -/
76	
77	/-- `L.instrs` has `is` as its segment starting at byte offset `pos`
78	    (`pos` 4-aligned; each `is[j]` is `L.instrs[pos/4 + j]`). Static in `L`; the
79	    machine fetch is recovered from `Installed` (a `StInv` conjunct). -/
80	def Emitted (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is : List Instr) : Prop :=
81	  pos % 4 = 0 ∧ ∀ (j : Nat) (h : j < is.length),
82	    ∃ (h2 : pos / 4 + j < L.instrs.length), L.instrs[pos / 4 + j] = is[j]'h
83	
84	/-- `Emitted` of a concatenation splits: `is₁` at `pos`, `is₂` at `pos + 4|is₁|`. -/
85	theorem Emitted_append_left (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is₁ is₂ : List Instr)
86	    (h : Emitted L pos (is₁ ++ is₂)) : Emitted L pos is₁ := by
87	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := h
88	  refine ⟨ha, fun j hj => ?_⟩
89	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf j (by simp only [List.length_append]; omega)
90	  exact ⟨h2, by rw [he, List.getElem_append_left hj]⟩
91	
92	theorem Emitted_append_right (L : Layout) (pos : Nat) (is₁ is₂ : List Instr)
93	    (h : Emitted L pos (is₁ ++ is₂)) : Emitted L (pos + 4 * is₁.length) is₂ := by
94	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := h
95	  refine ⟨by omega, fun j hj => ?_⟩
96	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf (is₁.length + j) (by simp only [List.length_append]; omega)
97	  have hpos : (pos + 4 * is₁.length) / 4 + j = pos / 4 + (is₁.length + j) := by omega
98	  have hb : (pos + 4 * is₁.length) / 4 + j < L.instrs.length := by rw [hpos]; exact h2
99	  refine ⟨hb, ?_⟩
100	  simp only [hpos, he]
101	  simp
102	
103	/-- The fetch bridge: `Emitted` (static) + `Installed` (a `StInv` conjunct,
104	    preserved across steps) ⇒ the machine at `codeBase + (pos + 4j)` decodes to
105	    `is[j]`. This is how the op proofs read each instruction from memory. -/
106	theorem decode_emitted (L : Layout) (m : State) (pos : Nat) (is : List Instr)
107	    (hem : Emitted L pos is) (hinst : Installed L m) (j : Nat) (hj : j < is.length) :
108	    decode (fetch32 { m with pc := L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (pos + 4 * j) }) = is[j]'hj := by
109	  obtain ⟨ha, hf⟩ := hem
110	  obtain ⟨h2, he⟩ := hf j hj
111	  obtain ⟨hcode, _⟩ := hinst
112	  have hpc4 : L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (pos + 4 * j)

**Completed Phase 1 (encode/decode round-trip) — the self-contained, no-dependency next step:**
- New file `lean/LowIR/ProgSim/EncodeFacts.lean`: a general, **axiom-clean** `decode (encode i) = i` for **all 16 RV64I forms**, plus `WF : Instr → Prop` and the dispatcher `decode_encode : ∀ i, WF i → decode (encode i) = i`. This replaces the per-program `native_decide` round-trips (which carry `Lean.ofReduceBool`) that Phase 2's `Installed`-from-`progBytes` would otherwise need.
- **The engine** (route (b), since `bv_decide` is unavailable and the stdlib's `and_two_pow_sub_one_eq_mod` is `Classical.choice`-tainted): a clean bit toolkit culminating in `lor_window_add`, an **order-agnostic "disjoint OR is addition"** combinator. It reduces `(encode i).toNat` to an arithmetic sum, so every `field` extraction closes by `omega` — even the scattered S/B/J immediates.
- **Two real side conditions surfaced** (now captured in `WF`, both Phase-2 obligations): `slli`/`srli` only round-trip for `sh < 32` (the trusted decode's `funct7 = field w 25 7` overlaps shamt bit 5), and branches/`jal` need even (4-aligned) offsets.

**Bookkeeping:** three commits (`b4a37a1`, `d2b4a80`, `86dd2be`), `w32`/`encB`/`encJ` un-privated in the frozen `Core.lean` (visibility only), docs + frontier memory updated, `docs/check_docs.py` and the full `lake build` both green.

**Next (Phase 2, `AsmFacts.lean`):** prove `Installed`-from-`progBytes` via `decode_encode`, and discharge the flat obligations (`hdat`/`hdbase`/`hdpos`/`hpad`/`hfn`/`halign`/`hstackLo` + the `WF` conditions) from the real layout pipeline — then Phase 6 `prog_sim` composes it all. 229	/-- Pass A over one stream from byte position `pos`: positioned items,

231	def layoutItems : List SymInstr → Nat → List (Nat × SymInstr) × List (Nat × Nat) × Nat
232	  | [], pos => ([], [], pos)
233	  | .label l :: rest, pos =>
234	      let (flat, lbls, pos') := layoutItems rest pos
235	      ((pos, .label l) :: flat, (l, pos) :: lbls, pos')
236	  | si :: rest, pos =>
237	      let (flat, lbls, pos') := layoutItems rest (pos + symSize si)
238	      ((pos, si) :: flat, lbls, pos')
239	
240	/-- Pass A: walk the whole program, assigning byte positions. Returns the flat
241	    positioned stream, the label→addr map, the function→addr map, and the end
242	    position (total code bytes). -/
243	def layout : List (Name × List SymInstr) →
244	    (start : Nat) →
245	    List (Nat × SymInstr) × List (Nat × Nat) × List (Name × Nat) × Nat
246	  | [], pos => ([], [], [], pos)
247	  | (n, items) :: rest, pos =>
248	      let (flat1, lbls1, pos') := layoutItems items pos
249	      let (flat2, lbls2, fns, endP) := layout rest pos'
250	      (flat1 ++ flat2, lbls1 ++ lbls2, (n, pos) :: fns, endP)
251	
252	/-- Resolve one positioned symbolic instruction (range-checked). `cref d`:
253	    `jal T0, +4` reads the pc (T0 := address of the next instruction — pc-read
254	    with NO auipc, staying inside the 16-encoding surface and keeping the blob
255	    position-independent), then a fixed 3-instr synth of the delta to the data
256	    object into T1, then `add T0, T0, T1`. -/
257	def resolveOne (lbls : List (Nat × Nat)) (fns : List (Name × Nat))
258	    (dats : List (Name × Nat)) :
259	    Nat × SymInstr → Option (List Instr)
260	  | (_,   .label _)      => some []
261	  | (_,   .ins i)        => some [i]
262	  | (pos, .cref d)       => do

57	deriving Repr
58	
59	/-- Build the `Layout` for `entry` at `codeBase` straight from the FROZEN
60	    compiler. `none` iff the program is uncompilable (same condition as
61	    `compileProgT`). `segStart` is recomputed here from the resolved stream
62	    and is DEFINITIONALLY the compiler's `pad8 codeEnd` (proved in Phase 2). -/
63	def layoutOf (P : Program) (entry : Name) (codeBase stackLo : Word) : Option Layout :=
64	  match compileProgT P entry with
65	  | some (is, fns, _) =>
66	      some { codeBase, instrs := is, fnTab := fns,
67	             segStart := pad8 (4 * is.length), data := P.data, stackLo }
68	  | none => none
69	
70	/-- The machine halt pc: the self-loop landing pad the compiler puts at
71	    `codeBase+4` (the entry stub is `jal ra,entry` at `codeBase`; the entry
72	    returns to `codeBase+4`). Run the machine with `runFuel L.haltPc`. -/
73	def Layout.haltPc (L : Layout) : Word := L.codeBase + 4
74	
75	/-- Address one past the last instruction — the code window's top. -/
76	def Layout.codeEnd (L : Layout) : Word :=
77	  L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * L.instrs.length)
78	
79	/-- `Installed L m` — the compiled blob is faithfully present in `m`'s memory:
80	    (code) the 32-bit word at `codeBase + 4j` decodes via the TRUSTED
81	    `Rv64i.decode` to `instrs[j]`; (data) the const-data byte at
82	    `codeBase + segStart + i` equals `dataSegment data`'s byte `i`
83	    (`Prog.dataSegment_at` already relates that segment to the object bytes). -/
84	def Installed (L : Layout) (m : State) : Prop :=
85	  (∀ (j : Nat) (h : j < L.instrs.length),
86	      Rv64i.decode (Rv64i.fetch32 { m with pc := L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * j) })
87	        = L.instrs[j]'h)
88	  ∧ (∀ (i : Nat) (h : i < (dataSegment L.data).length),
89	      m.mem (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (L.segStart + i)) = (dataSegment L.data)[i]'h)
90	
91	/-! ### Computable shadows of `Installed` (the #guard oracle). -/
92	
93	/-- Every instruction slot decodes correctly (computable form of `Installed`'s
94	    code conjunct). -/
95	def codeInstalledB (L : Layout) (m : State) : Bool :=
96	  (List.range L.instrs.length).all fun j =>
97	    Rv64i.decode (Rv64i.fetch32 { m with pc := L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (4 * j) })
98	      == L.instrs.getD j .unknown
99	
100	/-- Every data byte is present (computable form of `Installed`'s data conjunct). -/
101	def dataInstalledB (L : Layout) (m : State) : Bool :=
102	  (List.range (dataSegment L.data).length).all fun i =>
103	    m.mem (L.codeBase + BitVec.ofNat 64 (L.segStart + i))
104	      == (dataSegment L.data).getD i 0

111	    IL store lands in the machine's private bytes (code, spill slots, saved ra,

## Summary

**Phase 2 (`AsmFacts.lean`) — started; `Installed`-from-memory complete, axiom-clean.**

Two commits (`479035e`, `16569ad`) delivering the whole "bytes → decode → `Installed`" chain that composes with Phase 1's `decode_encode`:

- **`fetch32_reassemble`** — `fetch32`'s little-endian recombination of a 32-bit word's four `insBytes` is that word (getLsbD extensionality, 4 windows).
- **`fetch32_encode`** — memory holding the four `insBytes i` ⇒ `fetch32 = encode i`.
- **`asmBytes_getElem?` / `asmBytes_getD` / `asmBytes_length`** — `asmBytes` is a flatten of length-4 blocks; byte `4j+k` is `insBytes instrs[j]` byte `k`.
- **`installed_code_of_mem`** — `Installed`'s **code conjunct**: `decode(fetch32 at codeBase+4j) = instrs[j]` for every slot, from a memory holding `asmBytes L.instrs` (given every emitted instruction is `WF`). Composes the byte-indexing + reassembly + Phase-1 `decode_encode`.
- **`installed_of_mem`** — full `Installed L m` (both conjuncts) from decoupled code/data byte hypotheses a `loadMem`-style memory supplies directly.
- **`userPad_eq`** — discharges the `call` case's `hpad` flat obligation (`userPad env g = userOff gd`).

All `[propext, Quot.sound]`. `AsmFacts.lean` wired into `LowIRProgSim`; full `lake build` (62 jobs) and `docs/check_docs.py` both green; frontier memory and `RESUME-PROGSIM.md` updated.

**Remaining Phase 2** (the hard structural core, cleanly scoped for a fresh session):
1. **`hfn`/`hem` — the layout↔`Emitted` correspondence**: prove `L.instrs` (= `compileProgT`'s resolved stream) carries `prologueI gd ++ emitCF … gd.body ++ epilogueI gd` at `fnPos g` — i.e. `compileFun`/`layout`/`resolveOne` equals the proof-side emit. This is the ~500-line structural induction (validated per-program by the `matchesRealProg` `#guard`s).
2. **Data-layout obligations** `hdbase`/`hdpos`/`hdat` (`dpos = segStart + dataOffsetsFrom`, `dbase = dbaseOf`; the `dataOffsetsFrom_shift`/`dataSegment_at` lemmas already exist).
3. **Trivial** `halign` (codeBase alignment from `SimPre`), `hstackLo` (`= L.stackLo`).

Then Phase 6 (`prog_sim`) composes `lower_sim_cf` on the entry body + entry-stub step + halt-pad bridge, discharging all the flat obligations from these.
